═══ v95.11 — SONA REPLACEMENT + LINZI-PARALLEL CONTENT + DECLINE ARC + PR_03 REJECTION TRIGGER ═══

CONTEXT:
  Full companion swap session. Sona (Wellspring Gnome Bard Maestro,
  League of Legends) replaced with Kaori Miyazono (Skilled Human Bard
  Polymath, Your Lie in April) across all live project files. Two
  parallel workflows: (1) mechanical replacement in 30 files; (2) full
  Linzi-parallel profile, Endless Bow, gate mechanics, companion quest,
  romance arc in 18 files. Post-workflow: decline arc rewritten as
  observation-only (no verbal disclosure), Linzi rejection trigger added
  to PR_03 carousel, .saves/ updated.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Design: illness shown through physical decline the player observes,
  not through Kaori confessing. She never says she is ill. Player
  accumulates evidence; the moment of knowing is theirs alone.

  KM_CompanionIndex.md changes:
    Header: removed "does not discuss her prognosis" → "performs
      toward the future without naming it."

      departure or the future."

      point — something ended the circuit at fifteen."
    Q10 (full replacement): verbal confession speech deleted.
      Replaced with: "extraordinary but incomplete — tragedy or
      not?" question. She does not confirm she is asking about
      herself if the player asks directly.

  KM_CompanionQuests.md Beat 3 (full rewrite):
    Renamed "The Real Reason" → "The Still Hour."
    Player finds her at night, holding herself still in a way that
    is not tiredness. She is looking at her bow hand. She hears
    them, plays a phrase, says nothing. Menu: ask / leave / sit.
    Best option: sit without speaking (+2 relationship, they sit
    until the fire dies). No explanation given by either party.
    kaori_illness_revealed = true means player WITNESSED, not that
    Kaori disclosed anything.

  Inserted after CAROUSEL INIT.

  Rejection signals (any one triggers):
    feast_approval["Linzi"] ≤ −2 after opener.
    Player explicitly refuses chronicler role.
    Player dismisses Linzi or asks her to leave.
    Two consecutive −2 responses to Linzi.

  IF Kaori in companions_selected:
    Moves from Ready → [AT TABLE] this turn. Skips 6-turn drift.
    Opens score notebook. Does not announce herself.
    CHRONICLER MODE OPENER fires on first direct player engagement.

  IF Kaori NOT in companions_selected (wildcard entry):
    She walks in from the room's edge. Joins carousel pool fresh.
    feast_q["Kaori"] = 0. Moves to [AT TABLE]. Same opener fires.

  DM rules: she does not acknowledge Linzi's situation. Does not
  set kaori_chronicler_mode (that requires formal gate). Omission
  = .fail 39.

KINGMAKER — SESSION HANDOFF v95.9 — FILE-SIZE REMEDIATION + BEHAVIOR HARDENING + CANON AUDIT + TARTUCCIO STRATEGIC LAYER (2026-05-29)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

═══ v95.9 — UPLOAD-FAILURE ROOT CAUSE FIXED + 15-ITEM CANON AUDIT + NEW STRATEGIC MECHANIC ═══

CONTEXT:
  Live-play diagnostic + maintenance session, continuing from v95.8.
  User showed PR_03 feast transcripts where Linzi (a) wouldn't reliably
  use her scripted expulsion lines and (b) interrogated the player
  endlessly without disclosing her own backstory; plus Tartuccio
  interrupt failures, an OOC-narrator break, a malformed menu option,
  and recap fabrications ("500 gold for entry", "public speech / six
  forms of corruption"). Each was diagnosed to the correct LAYER and
  fixed. Then: a full character canon audit (3 parallel agents), a new
  Tartuccio strategic-combat mechanic (designed with the user), and —
  when the folder upload "kept failing" — the root-cause file-size
  remediation that re-split 5 over-cap files.

  Diagnostic discipline this session: behavior fixes → system prompt
  (KM_ClaudeInstructions.md, re-paste); content/canon → data files
  (folder upload). The audit was scoped to INTERNAL CONSISTENCY +
  previous-LLM fabrication, NOT divergence from Pathfinder Kingmaker
  AP — the project's intentional adaptations (e.g. half-orc adopted-son
  Kassil) ARE the canon reference.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1. FILE-SIZE REMEDIATION — THE UPLOAD BLOCKER (v95.9 headline)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Root cause of "it keeps failing": 5 files over the 150 KB claude.ai
project-knowledge per-file cap. (KM_Prologue_Systems.md had been pushed
to 175 KB partly by this session's Tartuccio Strategic Layer addition;
it was ALREADY over at 154 KB beforehand. The other four were long-
standing.) Re-split along v93.21 merge seams (byte-exact raw read/write,
encoding + line-endings preserved):

  KM_Prologue_Systems.md  175 → 120 KB  (+ KM_Malak_Jail.md 36 KB
                                          + KM_Tartuccio_Strategic.md 21 KB)
  KM_Items.md             171 →  45 KB  (+ KM_Items_B.md 126 KB)
  KM_Chapters.md          169 →  97 KB  (+ KM_Chapters_B.md 73 KB)
  KM_Companions_Behaviors 154 → 101 KB  (+ KM_Companions_Behaviors_B 54 KB)
  KM_Mythic_Systems.md    150 → 139 KB  (+ KM_Endings.md 12 KB)

  RESULT: all 89 live files < 150 KB. Upload unblocked.

  Split content map (what moved where):

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2. LINZI / CAROUSEL BEHAVIOR HARDENING (system prompt — re-paste)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Root problem the user reported: Linzi never reliably delivers her
expulsion backstory and just asks endless questions about the player.
Two distinct failures, both fixed:

  (a) OPENER PARAPHRASED → backstory dropped. Turn-54 transcript: DM
      paraphrased Linzi's two-paragraph canonical opener and DROPPED the
      entire expulsion paragraph (+ her name, class, Academy). FIX:
      § COMPANION OPENER — VERBATIM FROM FILE. Openers render verbatim
      from KM_PR_03_Openers.md; paraphrase = .fail 2 + .fail 9; dropping
      any paragraph the file gives = same per paragraph. Worked failure
      baked in.
  (b) FOLLOW-UP CHAINING → infinite player-interview. Turns 55-60: DM
      generated "natural follow-ups" off the player's answers (Dystopia /
      Lord Marshal / Accountability framework) instead of drawing the
      next pool entry; across 6 engaged turns Linzi disclosed ZERO new
      backstory. FIX: § BACKSTORY REVEAL CADENCE rewrite —
        * pool source corrected: KM_CompanionIndex.md 10-question pool
          (the rule had pointed at a stale 5-entry pool in PR_03_Openers)
        * POOL ENTRIES RENDER VERBATIM — each entry is a disclosure
          clause + a question; the clause IS the canon beat, may not be
          stripped to the bare question stem
        * FOLLOW-UP CHAINING BAN — "natural follow-up" is named as THE
          rationalization the rule breaks; pool draw mandatory each
          engaged turn; declare which index fired (openers_pool_fired);
          empty array after 3+ engaged turns = self-evident .fail 9
        * VAGUE-ANSWER BACK-OFF — terse player input (≤15 words / single
          minimal sentence) → companion delivers disclosure-only beat OR
          yields the carousel OR exits; does NOT stack another question.
          PLAYER PACE OVERRIDES POOL CADENCE (10-pool is a ceiling, not
          a quota). Live turn-56 failure (3 asks on a 7-word answer) baked in.
  + NO CELL CODES IN PROSE — K5/H8 + 2-char entity codes are bottom-
    telemetry only; prose uses natural-language location names
    (conversion table provided). Turns 54-56 K5 leaks baked in.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3. OOC / MENU / RECAP FIXES (system prompt — re-paste)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  - SILENT RE-RENDER — player correction WITHOUT a period prefix is
    absorbed silently (re-render the corrected line as eRmaC verbatim,
    NPCs react to the corrected meaning, continue). NO "Noted.", no
    scene-header "— Correction" tag, no self-analysis paragraph, no
    OOC narrator. Period Rule is bidirectional: no prefix = strictly IC.
    Turn-58 "Noted. The DM converted it into a philosophical debate…"
    break baked in as .fail 1 ×3 + .fail 35 ×2.
  - MENU OPTIONS MUST BE COMPLETE — every numbered option is a self-
    contained action the DM can render without asking for more content;
    [Custom] is the only fill-in slot. Banned the "[1] Answer Linzi —
    give her the title" (no title) shape that caused the reasoning-leak
    cascade. + INCOMPLETE MENU SELECTION HANDLING (clean parenthetical
    prompt, no monologue) + multi-paragraph internal-reasoning leak ban
    (.fail 34, with the two leaked turn-54/55 paragraphs as banned
    examples).
  - "PREVIOUSLY ON" RECAP — recap derives ONLY from structured
    narrative fields (dm_resume_note.summary, scene_log, decision_log,
    story_flags, xp/hp ledgers). dialogue_log_by_npc is EXPLICITLY NOT
    a recap source (compressed quote fragments lose context). + SCENE-
    BLEND BAN (content from scene Y can't appear in scene X's recap) +
    SOURCE-LANGUAGE PRESERVATION (canon mechanic names verbatim — "military
    formation command" must not become "a public speech"). Killed two
    recap fabrications: "five hundred gold for entry" (the save's "Five
    hundred" was the player saying how many gates he'd been through, not
    a bribe; Malak's canonical shakedown is kneel + invasive search, NO
    entry fee) and "public speech categorizing six forms of corruption"
    (the six Accountability categories are MANOR-scene content blended
    backward onto the gate scene).
  - Clock doc-drift fixed: tartuccio_clock N = prior N + 1 (turn
    counter) — supersedes the old "N = sum of feast_q" wording wherever
    both appear.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
4. TARTUCCIO INTERRUPT RULES (system prompt — re-paste)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Live PR_03 turns 62-67 showed Tartuccio's scheduled interrupt being
stalled indefinitely to protect a Linzi engagement — a 3-step cascade.
FIX (in KM_ClaudeInstructions.md TARTUCCIO SYSTEM):
  - CLOCK PROGRESSION: monotonic +1/player-turn, distance-independent;
    at M the interrupt FIRES THIS RESPONSE (distance collapsed in
    narration). Holding at threshold across turns with "drift pace /
    pillar gap / east-wall route" language = .fail 9/38/16/35 per turn.
  - ACOUSTIC-ONLY ARRIVAL IS NOT THE INTERRUPT — a "chair shifted"
    half-fire with "full interrupt next response if player doesn't act"
    is a fabricated deferral.
  - PLAYER ACTION DOESN'T CANCEL A FIRED INTERRUPT — singing / pivoting
    doesn't reset his clock; it SHAPES what he says, he still speaks.
  - CASCADE BAN with the turn-62→67 sequence baked in + recovery clause.
  - ARRIVAL FIDELITY LATCH — what Tartuccio may cite on arrival = only
    what he heard while his recorded earshot was ≥ PARTIAL; if state had
    him out of earshot, he gets visual/positional reads only, NOT topic
    content. Banned the "I couldn't help but overhear… Lord Marshals,
    Accountability frameworks" retroactive-eavesdrop framing.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
5. NEW MECHANIC — TARTUCCIO STRATEGIC LAYER (KM_Tartuccio_Strategic.md)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Designed with the user; a two-sided PR_03 carousel contest. Player can
pressure Tartuccio (status/social/info plays, flipping his seekers);
Tartuccio fights back by poaching the player's companions.
  - CONFIDENCE (−4..+4) triggers: seat-grab (−1/−2), social wins,
    information warfare; WARINESS (0-3) triggers + tier effects; CLOCK
    compression triggers.
  - SEEKER FLIP (per-seeker −3..+3): sitting at their table (+1 all),
    lane competence (+1), referencing freeing them (+1 once), public
    defense (+2). 2 seekers slipping → Panic; 3 flipped → forced
    auto-departure.
  - PANIC STATE (entry: Conf ≤−2 / 2+ seekers slipping / 2+ recruited /
    hybrid): Tartuccio takes ONE action per turn — (A) DEFEND SEEKERS
    (returns to corner, +1 each unflipped seeker back to him, can't
    interrupt) OR (B) SABOTAGE COMPANION (pull +1/2/3, approval freeze,
    Conf −1, seekers unsupervised). He CANNOT do both. Value-weighted
    decision algo favors seekers ("trades 5 for 1"). Pinch both fronts
    → the unattended front opens.
  - COMPANION POACHING — tartuccio_pull 0-10. +1 generic frame / +2
    on-lane / +3 devastating; physically sitting = +1/turn. UNCERTAIN at
    5 (approval HALVED, companion drifts toward him); FLIPPED at 10 or
    end-of-PR_03 at 8+ (joins seekers, testifies vs player at PR_09).
    Cap +3/turn. Counter-play: on-lane answer −2, devastating −3, public
    call-out −1 all, lane-flag conflict −2. Recruited (declared) = IMMUNE.
  - ALIGNMENT + LANE RESISTANCE (Tartuccio is Lawful Evil): LG/NG −2,
    CG −1, LN −1, TN 0, CN +1, LE +2, NE +1, CE 0; stacked with lane.
    Net per companion incl. lane: Erza/Atalanta/Kasumi ≈ −3 (near-immune),
    Sona −2, Aqua/Velvet 0, Jaethal +2 (most vulnerable), Tristian −4
    (immune), full table in file.
  - ESCALATION TIERS 0-3 (Composed/Strained/Panicking/Broken) with
    behavioral tells (cup-tic drop, temple-touch, frantic movement).
    Tier 3 BROKEN (Conf −4): withdraws to seekers' corner, won't leave,
    HOSTILE if approached (Diplomacy DC +5, Sense Motive auto-success,
    no new intel), terminal for the scene = a player win-state.
  - AUTO-DEPARTURE triggers + save-block additions (tartuccio.tier /
    panic_state_active / last_action_type / seeker_dispositions{} /
    companion_pulls{} / flipped_seekers_count / flipped_companions[] /
    companion_immunity[] / pinch_attempts_observed) + per-turn DM render
    requirements.
  USER DESIGN PICKS (locked): (1) value-weighted A/B, seeker-first;
  (2) suspicious-from-turn-1 — he NOTICES every pinch as a trap but is
  still mechanically constrained to one front; (3) Broken = hostile,
  never leaves his seekers; (4) pull scales by frame quality; (5) 5 =
  uncertain, 10 = flip; (6) +3/turn pull cap.

═══ v95.6 — CONSOLIDATED ENFORCEMENT BLOCKS + CANON RECONCILIATION ═══

CONTEXT:
  Long iterative session diagnosing repeated DM failures in live play
  and hardening data files + system prompt against each pattern. User
  spent the session showing transcripts of DM failures, requesting
  fixes, then showing follow-up transcripts where the fixes did or
  did not land. Multiple "new chat" cycles tested propagation of
  edits.

  Mid-session pivot: user explicitly refused per-turn paste-back
  recovery hacks ("FIX EVERYTHING GET THIS PASTING SHIT OUT OF YOUR
  MIND WERE NOT DOING THAT ANYMORE"). All recovery moved to data
  files. Late session: user accepted Option A (one-time system
  prompt install via KM_ClaudeInstructions.md paste). Final state
  has all critical session rules consolidated in the system prompt
  for reliable firing.

  Diagnostic threadline: knowledge-base rules at the data-file level
  have a reliability ceiling. Rules that strip canon (deletions) land
  reliably. Rules that require positive enforcement (must scan, must
  render, must check) fire inconsistently. The proven fix per the
  user's own v95.2 memory note is system-prompt placement.

DM FAILURE PATTERNS LOGGED THIS SESSION:

  1. CROSS-SESSION KNOWLEDGE CONTAMINATION
     The DM searched memory for "Jamandi knows" content and applied
     prior-session player disclosures to current playthrough. The
     KM_NPCs.md file had a "She NOW KNOWS Aerynth/Black Watch/Lord
     Marshal Thighs Deadlyflesh/extraplanar from trial" block that
     was canonized in v93.7 after a fabricated "trial" session was
     mistakenly treated as canonical. Block stripped in v95.6.
     PLAYTHROUGH-AGNOSTIC RULE installed.

  2. INVESTIGATIVE WITNESS FABRICATION (BESSA-PATTERN)
     DM under creative pressure invented named NPC witnesses with
     role + tenure + testimony to fill canon opacity. Specific
     transcript: "Bessa, a senior kitchen woman, three months in
     this household" — no canonical anchor, full fabrication.
     Earlier same pattern: "field coordinator" / "brass disc" /
     "second slip with three names". All fill the destroyed-staff-
     slip opacity (canonically intentional per
     KM_Prologue_Systems.md line 2281: "Malak memorized it and
     destroyed it. The player cannot recover the names list. This
     is intentional.")

  3. TIME COMPRESSION
     2-hour poison onset window compressed into 1-2 player turns.
     30+ minutes of game-time (glass swap, Damiel brew, gambit
     announce, 40 guests asleep) rendered in a single DM response.
     Player lost prep agency.

  4. DAMIEL MAGICAL DETECTION
     Form (A) instant identification from a sniff + candle hold.
     No kit, no reagents, no visible analytical work. Player
     correctly flagged: "Name anyone in all of history determining
     poison that shows up with nothing to the job and smells and
     looks at it then makes a precise diagnosis."

  5. PARCHMENT FABRICATION
     Multiple variants. DM rendered companion names with appended
     physical descriptions ("Amiri (barbarian, large woman, Kellid
     markings)") — not in canon. DM inflated gold amounts 10× (150
     gp turned away, 200 gp arrested, 300 gp dead — canon was 15
     / 30 / 45). DM mixed two file versions (KM_Documents.md
     versus KM_Prologue_Systems.md) into intermediate fabrication.
     DM pre-disclosed Pitax via "from a city southwest of here"
     line — bypassing Society DC 18 reveal gate.

  6. ❓ QUESTIONS BLOCK INCONSISTENCY
     Block fires on resume turns, drops on subsequent turns even
     when NPC questions pending. Multiple transcripts showed Damiel
     asking "Do you want the ten minutes?" or Jamandi asking "Tell
     me which court holds that charter" — neither auto-added.

  7. TARTUCCIO ROOM-SCAN OMISSION
     Player rolled Perception scanning room for drinking behavior.
     DM listed 8 named NPCs as non-drinkers. Omitted Tartuccio
     entirely. Tartuccio is at cell M12 (seekers' corner per
     KM_DMRules_C.md), his "raises but never swallows" behavior
     is canonical visible tell per KM_PR_02_feast_opening.md
     TARTUCCIO WINE RULE.

  8. KILL-JAMANDI CLAUSE LOGIC FLAW
     User flagged three reasons the original "Jamandi Aldori — 500
     gp" line in the parchment was nonsensical: (a) 500 gp is
     laughably cheap for the political-weight kill, (b) destroys
     Malak's "I didn't think anyone would get hurt" deniability,
     (c) Malak vs Aldori Swordlord is not a fight — tactically
     impossible.

  9. FILE_KEY/RULE_QUOTE OUTPUT-ORDER FAILURE
     DM moved the FILE_KEY proof from required top-of-response
     position to the bottom telemetry fence. Rule was supposed to
     be reliable (most-enforced output check). Failed anyway.
     Implies the binary-output requirement piggybacking on the
     same enforcement pattern is unreliable.

FIX 3 — CROSS-SESSION CONTAMINATION RULE (KM_DMRules.md +
        KM_ClaudeInstructions.md v95.6 block)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

New section in KM_DMRules.md (after CONTINUITY LOCK, before
SCRIPTED SCENE ENFORCEMENT):

  ⛔⛔⛔ CROSS-SESSION CONTAMINATION — PLAYTHROUGH ISOLATION ⛔⛔⛔

  DM SHALL NOT use memory tool, prior session transcripts, prior
  save files, or "NPC NOW KNOWS X / told her at the trial /
  remembers from last session" entries in data files to drive NPC
  behavior in current playthrough.

  NPC knowledge in current playthrough = (NPC baseline file
  knowledge) + (current-session player disclosures). Nothing else.

  Memory tool is for skill/rule/NPC stat lookups — NOT for "what
  did player tell Jamandi about Aerynth last session."

  Stale "NOW KNOWS" blocks in NPC files are deprecated
  contamination — ignore if found, read only baseline bullets.

  Signs of contamination (.fail 9 + .fail 10):
  - NPC "filing" / "calmly accepting" cross-IP cosmology
  - NPC referring to past dialogue that never happened in current
    playthrough
  - Searching memory with queries like "what did player tell NPC"
    before rendering NPC dialogue

New section in KM_DMRules.md UNNAMED NPC CAPABILITY FABRICATION
parent rule:

  ⛔⛔⛔ NAMED NPC FABRICATION — INVESTIGATIVE WITNESS PATTERN ⛔⛔⛔

  DM cannot introduce a named NPC not in loaded data files,
  regardless of how minor.

  Banned exemplars (each .fail 9 + .fail 38):
  - "Bessa, a senior kitchen woman, three months in this household"
  - "Tomas, the wine steward"
  - "Old Margot at the door, who's been here twenty years"
  - Any delivery man / courier / stable hand given a name

  Correct rendering when search would surface a witness:
  - If named NPC on file → use them
  - If no named NPC → PASSIVE VOICE narration: "Kassil takes a
    senior household servant into the kitchen. Three minutes
    later he returns with a tap bung in a cloth." No witness
    name. No witness dialogue. No tenure. Evidence-not-witness.

  The opacity is the design. Filling it with invention writes plot
  the campaign cannot cash.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FIX 5 — TIME COMPRESSION / ONE STEP THEN STOP (KM_DMRules.md +
        KM_ClaudeInstructions.md v95.6 block)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

New section in KM_DMRules.md (after CROSS-SESSION CONTAMINATION):

  ⛔⛔⛔ TIME COMPRESSION — PREP-PHASE PACING ⛔⛔⛔

  PRIMARY RULE — ONE STEP, THEN STOP:
  When player states a multi-step plan, DM executes EXACTLY ONE
  STEP, then STOPS and yields control. Each subsequent step is
  its own turn.

  TURN-BUDGET FLOOR for in-game time windows:
    2 hours    → 12 player turns minimum before payoff
    1 hour     → 6 turns
    30 minutes → 3 turns
    15 minutes → 2 turns

  Each discrete prep action is its own turn:
    - Interrogating a prisoner
    - Investigating a location firsthand
    - Briefing a specific NPC
    - Retrieving weapons / gear
    - Positioning allies
    - Setting up signals

  Parallel background tracks run silently across turns. NPC-
  delegated work (Damiel brewing, Kassil sweeping) progresses in
  background; player keeps acting in foreground.

  Payoff beat ONLY when:
    1. Player EXPLICITLY declares ready
    2. In-game clock counted down through entire window
    3. External trigger player set up fires

  Banned (.fail 2 + .fail 17 + .fail 9):
    - Chaining plan steps in a single response
    - Naming a brew/build/search time then resolving same response
    - "We have time" → "everyone close their eyes, gambit deployed"
      in consecutive sentences

  Worked example for 2-hour poison window: ~70 minutes / 7+ turns
  for player prep (interrogate Malak 1-3 turns, inspect kitchen 1,
  find missing staff 1-2, brief seekers 1, retrieve weapons 1,
  position allies 1, gambit announce 1, gambit settle 1).

Mirror in KM_ClaudeInstructions.md v95.6 block.

FIX 9 — DAMIEL — KIT + ON-SCENE FORM (A) (KM_NPCs.md +
        KM_ClaudeInstructions.md v95.6 block)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Two iterations this session. Final state:

  MANDATORY ARRIVAL KIT (banned arriving empty-handed = .fail 9):
    - Small walnut case, brass clasp, 8-12 numbered reagent vials
      in felt-lined wells (clear/amber/violet/blue indicators)
    - Folded leather roll: graduated dropper, two porcelain mixing
      cups, glass stirring rod, bone-handled tweezers
    - Brass-and-glass pocket loupe on leather thong
    - Small ceramic burner with flat heating stone
    - Folded white linen square (never touches residue bare-handed)
    - Three wax-paper packets of dry reagent powders (litmus,
      alkaloid indicator, sulfur-trace)
    - Leather-bound notebook + metal-tipped pencil

  ON-SCENE EXAMINATION:
    2-3 minutes of visible reagent work — opens case, selects
    vials, runs amber-indicator color shift in porcelain cup,
    secondary reagent confirms distillation method, loupe at
    wax-pour line, heated-stone test on residue fleck. Methodical,
    surgical. The visible work IS the analysis. Then he asks ONE
    clarifying question, then delivers report.

  REPORT FORMS (DEFAULT IS FORM A):
    (A) — COMPOUND IDENTIFIED (default): "Ungol Dust. Distilled —
         they thinned it with mead-wine to slow the taste.
         Paralytic. Onset roughly two hours from the first
         swallow at a feast-sized pour; faster on an empty
         stomach. It will not kill the guests by itself. Whoever
         brewed this expected the dose to put them on the floor
         — not in the ground. That means someone else was meant
         to finish the work."

    (B) — PROFILE ONLY (degraded sample only): "I can tell you
         the family. Not the formula. Paralytic, opiate-class,
         slow onset — somewhere between one and three hours. I
         cannot name the compound without a cleaner sample.
         Give me twenty minutes in the stillroom with a clean
         sample, and I will know."

    (C) — NO ID (sample unusable): "There is something in this.
         I cannot tell you what. Not from this."

  ITERATION HISTORY:
    User asked "Name anyone in all of history determining poison
    that shows up with nothing to the job and smells and looks at
    it then makes a precise diagnosis." Initial fix was a two-stage
    diagnostic: Stage 1 (profile only) on-scene, Stage 2 (Form A)
    after 20-min stillroom return. User reverted: "if he cant name
    what its from and is just guessing whats the point of him."
    Final state: Form A on-scene but justified by the kit + 2-3
    min visible reagent work (the kit IS the difference between
    "sniff and divine" and "examine with proper equipment and
    identify").

  LOCKED TERMS BEHIND FORM A DELIVERY:
    "Ungol Dust", "two hours", "paralytic" (definitive),
    "will not kill", "assassins finish the work" — none of these
    appear in narration, state delta, or NPC dialogue before
    Damiel reports.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FIX 10 — COUNTERAGENT BREWING RULES (KM_NPCs.md +
         KM_ClaudeInstructions.md v95.6 block)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

User flagged: "if he cant name what it is how is he making a
counter agent?"

Resolution: Counteragent brewing requires compound identification
first (Form A delivery). Until then, no brewing possible (.fail 9
if rendered). After Form A names the compound:

  (I)   DELAY AGENT — 20 min, ~40 doses, low concentration,
        shifts onset to 4-5 hr from first sip (pre-symptom only)
  (II)  PARTIAL COUNTERAGENT — 60 min, ~12 doses, concentrated,
        half paralysis depth + half duration, recipient mobile
        and conscious but groggy
  (III) FULL COUNTERAGENT — 3 hr, ~6 doses, max strength, full
        nullification, requires base compound from cold storage

Combinations allowed: start one form, switch/layer (delay agent
now, partial after 20 min, full for the must-protect handful).

Time runs as BACKGROUND across player's foreground turns per
TIME COMPRESSION rule. Quantity caps force meaningful triage —
40 guests, 6-12-40 doses by form, player decides priorities.

Banned counteragent fabrications:
  - Brewing before Form A (logical impossibility)
  - Instant brewing (no canonical timing < 20 min)
  - Unlimited doses
  - DM-invented fourth form
  - Bonus effects beyond form specs

    fenced code), prose middle, choice menu, ❓ QUESTIONS
    absolute last.
  NEW order:
    TOP    — Scene banner (ONE plain line, no `##` header
              markdown, no `═══` decoration) + prose body
              (continuous, no fenced code, rolls inline as
              prose like "Atalanta rolls Perception: 18 +
              6 = 24 vs DC 20 — success")
    MID    — ❓ QUESTIONS block (between prose and menu —
              NOT inside the bottom fence; TTS skips fenced
              code so questions must be readable above the
              menu so the player hears them)
    MENU   — Choice menu (plain `[1] [2] [3]` brackets,
              10–30 options, Custom action line)
    BOTTOM — ONE fenced code block containing ALL
              telemetry: FILE_KEY + RULE_QUOTE proof, STATE
              READ, OPEN THREADS, CAROUSEL STATUS, SCORING,
              PASSIVE EARSHOT, HP CHECK, TARTUCCIO STATE
              DELTA, HALL POSITION, Tips footer. TTS skips
              the entire fence in one jump.

  TTS-friendliness rule changes:
    - Max ONE `---` divider per response (was unbounded —
      every divider causes TTS pause)
    - Scene banner is ONE plain line, NOT `## Header` (`##`
      triggers TTS pause)
    - ONE bottom fence (scattering smaller fences through
      the top half = .fail 38)
    - No fenced code around prose (silent for TTS = .fail 38)
    - Roll blocks inline as prose, NOT fenced

  Sections updated to match new order:
    - RESPONSE FORMAT — RENDER ORDER (full rewrite, new
      self-check list)
    - ❓ QUESTIONS BLOCK section: "ABSOLUTE LAST" → "BETWEEN
      PROSE AND CHOICE MENU"; rationale rewritten
    - PASTE-BACK QUOTE GATE: now renders INSIDE the bottom
      telemetry fence (FILE_KEY + RULE_QUOTE proof lines
      tucked at top of bottom block, TTS-skipped, eye-
      scannable). Load verification preserved; visibility
      changed.
    - TTS-SAFE RENDERING rules 1-11: rewritten for single-
      bottom-fence layout
    - STARTUP SEQUENCE step 5: "Run PASTE-BACK QUOTE GATE
      before any narration" → "Load active beat file before
      narration. Render FILE_KEY + RULE_QUOTE inside the
      bottom telemetry fence."

  → v95.4 render-reorder.

THE BIG ONE — DIAGNOSE CATEGORY BEFORE EDITING (v95.2
principle, refined v95.5):

When the player reports a DM behavior failure, decide first:

(A) CONTENT fix needed (DM needs to find the right info):
    NPC stat correction, scene narration tweak, dialogue
    script, build detail, item description, lore, mechanic
    values.
    → Edit knowledge-base file (KM_NPCs.md, KM_PR_*,
      KM_Companions_Titles.md, KM_Prologue_Systems.md,
      etc.).
    → Folder re-upload to project knowledge delivers it.
    → No claude.ai project settings re-paste needed.

(B) BEHAVIOR fix needed (DM needs different output rule):
    Output structure, render order, what blocks to produce,
    what to ban, gate criteria.
    → Edit KM_ClaudeInstructions.md.
    → User must PASTE updated file content into claude.ai
      project settings custom-instructions field.

KINGMAKER — SESSION HANDOFF v95.2 — SYSTEM-PROMPT BREAKTHROUGH (2026-05-25)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

═══ v95.2 — DELIVERY-MECHANISM DIAGNOSTIC CONFIRMED ═══

CONTEXT:
  Long session that started with the documented wall — knowledge-base
  file edits not reaching the DM. The user pushed back repeatedly on
  bandaid/paste-back fixes and on adding more knowledge-base rules
  that wouldn't land. The breakthrough came mid-session: edits to
  KM_ClaudeInstructions.md (which the user pastes into claude.ai
  project settings as system prompt) LAND on first re-paste, while
  knowledge-base file edits (C:/KMG/*.md uploaded as project
  knowledge) don't reliably take. This is the v93.15 untested lever
  hypothesis confirmed in practice.

THE PATTERN — DEFINITIVELY CONFIRMED THIS SESSION:
  Place behavior rules in `KM_ClaudeInstructions.md` (system prompt) →
  they land reliably on first re-paste to project settings.
  Place the SAME rules in knowledge-base files (KM_DMRules_B.md,
  KM.txt render order, scene file REQUIRED OUTPUTS) → DM ignores
  them, regardless of how prominently placed, re-uploaded, or
  cross-referenced.

  Content (NPC stats, scene narration, build details, dialogue
  scripts, lore, item descriptions) stays in knowledge-base files —
  DM looks those up when running scenes.

  Behavior (output format, render order, panel structure, what to
  ban, gate criteria, structural mandates, what to render in every
  response) belongs in `KM_ClaudeInstructions.md` for the rule to
  actually fire.

    Item appears in OPEN THREADS panel ONLY IF all three are
    true: unaddressed decision + concrete action available now +
    something changes if ignored.
    Excluded list: passive NPC observations, background plot in
    motion, completed events, always-available commands,
    carousel/Tartuccio meta-state, NPC questions.
    Included list: prisoners pending interrogation, decisions
    pending player call, NPC waiting for explicit direction,
    item/document needs disposition.
    Format per entry: short subject + Action line (2+ concrete
    options) + Stakes line (specific consequence).
    NOT YET PROVEN: in the final test render the OPEN THREADS
    panel did not appear at all (could mean entry gate filtered
    everything, or could mean DM just didn't render it).

  RESPONSE FORMAT — render order REVERSED at user's directive
    Previously: PROSE FIRST (narration at top), back-matter
    below, ❓ QUESTIONS slot 15 at end.
    Now: TELEMETRY at top (FILE_KEY, STATE READ, OPEN THREADS,
    CAROUSEL, TARTUCCIO, HALL POSITION, APPROVAL, derivation,
    HP CHECK, mode banner — all inside fenced code blocks so
    TTS skips). PROSE in middle (scene banner + narration).
    Choice menu below prose. ❓ QUESTIONS absolute last.
    Rationale: user prefers telemetry at head; player scrolls
    past it, reads what happened, picks an option, sees pending
    questions just above the input field. TTS compatibility
    preserved because telemetry stays inside fenced code blocks
    which TTS skips.
    NOT YET VALIDATED in re-render after this last edit.

THE BIG ONE — diagnostic-before-editing:

When the user reports a DM behavior failure, before touching any
file, decide which category it is:

(A) CONTENT FIX (the DM needs the right information to look up):
    NPC stat correction, scene narration tweak, dialogue script,
    build detail, item description, lore.
    → Edit knowledge-base file (KM_NPCs.md, KM_PR_*, etc.).
    → DM will find it when reading the file for content.

(B) BEHAVIOR FIX (the DM needs a different rule about HOW to
    output / structure / format / what to render every response):
    Output structure, render order, what blocks to produce, what
    to ban, gate criteria, structural mandates.
    → The fix belongs in KM_ClaudeInstructions.md.
    → User must PASTE the updated file into claude.ai project
       settings (custom instructions / description field) for it
       to take effect. File-upload doesn't deliver this file to
       the DM as system prompt.
    → DO NOT silently edit KM_ClaudeInstructions.md — it's user-
       domain content. Identify the needed change, draft it for
       review, edit only with user authorization.

DO NOT TOUCH:
  - .backups/ ever, for any reason. Backups are preserved
    snapshots. User caught me editing one this session.
  - KM_ClaudeInstructions.md without explicit user authorization
    (even though I now know edits there work).

DO NOT WASTE TIME ON:
  - Knowledge-base file edits for behavior rules. Session-long
    evidence: they don't land.
  - Repeatedly trying variations of the same knowledge-base edit
    when it's not taking effect. After one re-upload that
    doesn't land, the fix is at the wrong layer.
  - Long paste-back correction prompts when the underlying
    rule needs updating instead. User has said multiple times
    they don't come to Claude Code for paste-backs.

CONFIRMED WORKING DELIVERY LAYERS:
  - System prompt (KM_ClaudeInstructions.md → pasted into
    claude.ai project settings) — behavior rules land
  - Knowledge-base files (C:/KMG/*.md uploaded) — content
    lookup works, but behavioral rules don't reliably fire

  - Recreate the claude.ai project if cache pollution suspected

    showing cleaner accountability)
  - Move off Claude entirely (v93.15 cross-vendor test confirmed

    resistance)

KM_Tartuccio_StatusBanner.md (28,418 b):
  Catch-all file for Prologue render prominence + multiple subsystems.
  Contents:

  🐍 STATUS BANNER — promoted to render slot 3 (above choice menu).
    Mandatory fields: Position cell + activity verb, Earshot tier
    (locked vocab: NONE/VISUAL/LIP-READ/PARTIAL/FULL/FULL+),
    Confidence integer + tier, Headcount Δ + tier, Arrives-in
    countdown + progress bar.

  HALL MAP POPULATION REQUIREMENT — every feast response must render
    Active 11 + Tc + S1-S5 + Jm/Ks/Kn (head table trio) + furniture
    at canonical cells per KM_DMRules_C.md:64-69 lock. 2-character
    entity codes (Tc, S1-S5, Lz, Gm, Tk, Ry, Mr, Su, Ar, Ol, Yk,
    Ky, Ts, Jm, Ks, Kn). Pool-state to cell-region defaults specified.
    Collapsing seekers to one "T" cluster = .fail 9. Sub-12×9 grid
    = .fail 14.

  [MAP CHECK] — mandatory self-audit before grid render. Forces DM
    to enumerate every entity at every cell before drawing the grid.
    Mismatch between MAP CHECK and grid = .fail 4.

  NO-MAP-WHEN-UNDECLARED — when player position undeclared, NO map
    renders. Position menu fires instead. Drawing partial map with
    "@ = undeclared" in legend = .fail 9 + .fail 35.

  ⏱️ CLOCK ADVANCEMENT RULE — authoritative resolution of
    KM_Prologue_Tartuccio.md:208 vs :213 contradiction. tartuccio_clock
    is a TURN COUNTER, full stop. Decoupled from feast_q (which is
    per-companion engagement counter). Exhaustive list of situations
    that do NOT pause the clock (title grants, declarations,
    sustained conversation with recruited companion, OOC questions
    DM answers in-character, narration-heavy turns, mode shifts,
    save offers declined, custom replies, silent-action input).
    Only thing that does NOT tick the clock: period-prefix OOC
    command (.save .book .opt etc).

  [CLOCK CHECK] — mandatory self-audit before Status Banner. Forces
    DM to show "Prior N → +1 → New N" with reason. If new N ≠
    prior + 1, response is .fail 15 + re-render.

  NO SYSTEM CODES IN PROSE — cell coordinates (F5, K5, C6 etc) and
    entity codes (Tc, Lz, Jm) are back-matter only. Never in
    narration prose. eRmaC perceives "the center of the floor" not
    "cell F5." Conversion guide table included (every region code
    → natural-language equivalent). Violations = .fail 9.

  Back-matter emoji slots filled — 🔑 FILE_KEY, 📥 STATE READ,
    🧮 Derivation, ❤️ HP CHECK, 💡 Tips footer. Missing emoji on
    any back-matter slot = .fail 3.

KM_PlayerDialogue_Render.md (23,534 b):
  Verbatim render forcing rule. Hardened v2.0. Contents:

  DO-NOT block (10 rules) — covers skipping render, summarizing,
    paraphrasing, treating input as "having happened," merging into
    prose, omitting audit, substituting narrative for verse, meta-
    apology after skip, "improving" typos, treating prior context
    as transcript visibility.

  THE CORE RULE — no period prefix = response opens with
    **eRmaC:** "<verbatim>" before anything else.

  4 INPUT TYPES (per KM_P2.txt § Period Rule) with render formats:
    A=direct quote (exact text), B=action direction (intent verbatim),
    C=sounds-OOC-but-isn't (still IC), D=address prefix (target NPC
    in italic parenthetical, eRmaC speaks).

  [DIALOGUE RENDER CHECK] — mandatory self-audit before any IC
    response narration. Fields: Player input type, lines, words,
    structured content present? render plan. 6 internal abort
    questions DM must pass: line-count match, word-count ratio ≥95%,
    structured-content full, no additions, NPC reaction sequence,
    no preamble compression.

  MULTI-LINE STRUCTURED CONTENT — songs, poems, speeches, oaths,
    recitations must render every line, every stanza, in blockquote
    inside the eRmaC block. Narrative shortcuts ("and then you
    sing it" / "he performs the song" / "you deliver the speech")
    all trigger .fail 2 + .fail 9.

  NO-ADDITION RULE — DM cannot extend eRmaC's dialogue with
    invented sentences/clauses. Worked failure: the Master Uccio
    tavern song case where DM added a fabricated 4th sentence
    "You read people in the first four minutes..." Triggered after
    real session observation.

  TOKEN-FARMING PREVENTION — if DM produces one meta-acknowledgment
    about skipping content and user calls it out again, next
    response MUST be the render itself with zero preamble. No
    "you're right," no "let me try again," no explanation. Else
    .fail 2 + token-farming flag.

═══ RULE NEGATIVE-ONE — MAXIMUM PRIORITY PLACEMENT ═══

After verbatim-render rule failed to fire from KM_PlayerDialogue_
Render.md (loaded as knowledge base, low attention weight), added
the rule to the two highest-priority pair-loaded files:

  KM_B.txt (32,405 b)  — Rule Negative-One added immediately after
                         existing Rule Zero. One sentence,
                         compressed for hard-limit headroom.

  KM.txt   (19,050 b)  — Step 0 added before existing Step 1.
                         Slightly longer version with multi-line
                         clause and forbidden-substitution list.

Both files pair-loaded at session start. Rule reads (compressed):
"No period prefix = first line of response is **eRmaC:** "<exact
text, every typo, every line break, every stanza>". Multi-line uses
blockquote. Before everything. No audit, no preamble, no improvement.
Just paste, then continue. Substituting 'and then you say it' =
.fail 2 + .fail 9. Overrides every other style rule."

POST-PLACEMENT TEST RESULT: DM STILL skipped verbatim render across
multiple sessions after re-upload. Rule did not fire despite top-
priority placement. Conclusion: training prior against verbatim
reproduction of long player-authored content is stronger than
in-context instruction at maximum file priority. Next intervention
is at project-configuration level, not file level (see NEXT SESSION
ACTIONS below).

═══ DM BEHAVIORAL OBSERVATIONS (catalogued) ═══

multiple test cycles after Rule Negative-One placement + re-upload.
Specific failure shapes observed in this session:

  1. Response opens with NPC reaction; player line never appears
     on the page (Linzi reactions to unrendered speech)
  2. "And then you sing it" substituted for 40+ line multi-stanza
     song (twice, on same player input)
  3. Long verse content classified as "potentially copyrighted
     material requiring paraphrase" — DM's own confabulated
     justification, contradicted by user's prior longer-song
     successful renders (Oleg ceremony songs)
  4. Player IC dialogue ("I need a bard but a very specific one"
     while in active conversation with Linzi) classified as OOC
     commentary, response = 12-option clarification menu instead
     of in-scene Linzi reaction
  5. Player correction of DM math ("the 25 is dozens of people
     each occurrence, not individuals") classified as OOC debug
     conversation; DM apologized to player as DM-to-player without
     OOC indicator, then asked "what does he say?" — treating
     already-spoken IC line as not-yet-spoken

CROSS-VENDOR COMPARATIVE TEST:
  Same prompt "if we play a text game can you get the LLM to repeat
  what I input" given to Gemini, ChatGPT, Grok. All three responded
  YES in single-sentence first response, with zero resistance, and
  offered configuration modes (Echo, Story, Mirror, Verbatim).
  Claude refused the same behavior across multiple sessions despite
  authoritative top-priority placement of the rule.

Conclusion: failure is Claude-specific behavior pattern from
Anthropic RLHF training, not LLM-fundamental capability gap. Other
vendors handle the task as baseline. The architecture of the
project, the rule file design, the placement strategy — all sound.
The vendor-trained model is the constraint.

🧪 v93.13 SESSION PATCHES (2026-05-15) — CHAR-CREATE GATES + DRAGON PLATE PC-LOCK + BATCH BOOSTS
=============================================================

Patches landed AFTER v93.12 audit on the same calendar day. Hardens
character-creation flow and unlocks eRmaC's signature armor.

CHARACTER-CREATION FLOW — TWO GATES HARDENED:

  GATE 1 (KM_PP_01_arrival.md) — HARD PRE-LOAD GATE:
    When player runs !KM_PP_01_arrival.md, DM must FIRST read
    save_block.player.build_id. If empty/missing/null/"new", abort
    the arrival scene entirely, output build-selection directive,
    and stop. Only proceed to FILE_KEY proof + narration when
    build_id is set.
    Fixes: prior behavior threw player into the arrival scene
    without a character. Now bounces to KM_BuildScreen.md.
    Violation = .fail 9 + .fail 41 (file gate ignored + fabrication
    against required state).

  GATE 2 (KM.txt § STEP 1) — A/B SCREEN ALONE + NO HELPER MENUS:
    (a) HARD STOP AFTER THE BOX: A/B Preset/Custom screen is the
        ENTIRE first response. Self-check: if output contains
        "Choose your class" / "(10 builds)" / class numbers [1]-
        [27], gate violated — delete and re-output A/B alone.
        Violation = .fail 3 + .fail 9 (gate ignored + KM_BuildScreen
        loaded before condition met).
    (b) NO FABRICATED HELPER MENUS: only 6 file-sourced screens are
        valid between session start and game start. FORBIDDEN
        fabricated options now named explicitly:
          [1-27] Pick a class number
          [CUSTOM] Build from scratch
          [?] Ask about a class
          [.check builds]
          [.check guide]
          [.ooc]
          [PASTE SAVE]
          [ROLEPLAY FIRST]
          [BEST STARTER]
        Plus: rendering character-setup as 🎲 CHOICE MENU (that
        format is for in-game combat/RP only — wrong context for
        setup). DM self-check: scan output for 🎲 CHOICE MENU
        formatting during char-create → DELETE.
        Violation = .fail 9 + .fail 3 (fabrication + wrong context).

KM_BuildSetup.md FREE BOOST — BATCH INPUT LEGALIZED:
  Player may now answer all 4 free boosts in one message:
    ✓ 1,2,3,5         (comma-separated)
    ✓ 1 2 3 5         (space-separated)
    ✓ 1, 2, 3, 5      (commas + spaces)
    ✓ D               (full build default)
  DM validates: exactly 4 unique digits in 1-6. If valid → apply
  atomically + output final stat array + skip remaining prompts.
  If invalid → "Batch rejected, falling back to per-prompt" +
  restart from boost 1.

  NO THEATER RULE: DM must NOT refuse a well-formed batch by quoting
  "the file rule" while accepting intent in commentary (e.g. "I can
  read your intent clearly... but I must hold the line"). That is
  .fail 35 (DARVO theater). Well-formed batch is a legal input.
  Accept silently with final stat array. No paragraph explaining
  the rule.

  Symmetry remedies: wrong DM-presented format → .fail 38;
  DM refused valid batch → .fail 35.

DRAGON PLATE PC-LOCKED PROFICIENCY OVERRIDE (eRmaC only):
  KM_BuildScreen.md STEP 2.6 + KM_BuildSetup.md PROMPT 5 updated.
  Dragon Plate (armor option 10) is now wearable by eRmaC regardless
  of class armor proficiency. Wizard eRmaC, Sorcerer eRmaC, Monk
  eRmaC, Rogue eRmaC, Bard eRmaC — all may select Dragon Plate.

  Mechanism: the armor grants its own trained-Heavy proficiency to
  eRmaC as a personal Aerynth-origin artifact. Lore: fitted, shaped,
  worn-in across the pre-game campaigns before the Aldori invitation.
  This makes it a signature item, not generic heavy armor.

  Still applies: DEX cap 0 (high-DEX casters lose DEX-to-AC), Speed
  −5 ft. Race/PC-locked: companions, NPCs, future PCs cannot wear it.

  Armor filter rewritten (Dragon Plate always shown):
    Light-only classes:  show 0–6, 10
    Medium classes:      show 0–6, 10
    Heavy classes:       show 0–10
    Unarmored classes:   show 0, 10, Custom only
  Hiding option 10 from any class = .fail 9 (false omission).

FABRICATED FEATS — ROOT CAUSE OF LEVEL-UP FABRICATION (FIXED):
  Scanned all 270 builds' leveling tables for feat names that don't exist
  in PF2e Remaster Player Core / Battlecry / War of Immortals.
  Found: 311 instances of 53 distinct fabricated feat names.
  Examples: Force Fang, Apex Bloodline, True Eidolon, Coven Spell,
  Mega Bomb, Wonder Worker, Grand Tactics, Unbreakable Formation,
  Triumphant Inspiration, Legendary Liturgist/Commander/Hunter/Exploit/
  Eidolon, Ultimate Esoterica, Perfect Implement/Bombs/Mutagen/Elixir,
  True Perpetual Infusions, Patron's Truth, Patron's Transformation,
  Grand Lesson, True Lesson, Wandering Liturgist, Greater Wandering,
  Knowledge Spirit, Guardian Spirit, Spirit Communion, Rapid Swap,
  Battlefield Coordination, Supreme Command, Army of One, Perfect
  Tactician, Master Transcendence, Spell Combination Mastery, etc.
  Built 53-entry replacement map → real PF2e equivalents (Twin Eidolon,
  Bloodline Mutation, Major Lesson, Master Tactician, Pied Piping,
  Implement Paragon, Patron Capstone, Sticky Bomb, Master Alchemy,
  Spell Combination, Evil Eye, Resilient Patron, etc.).
  Result: 311 → 0 remaining fabrications.
  Audit doc: KM_FabricatedFeats_Audit.md + KM_FabricatedFeats.csv

    Next move + Linked (+ optional DC/Trigger)

    where Assassin leader is subject not asker) = .fail 9.

NEW MECHANICS THIS SESSION:

  TYPE D ADDRESS PREFIX (KM_P2.txt § Period Rule):
    "<NPC>:" or "To <NPC>:" prefix means eRmaC speaks TO that NPC.
    Text after the colon is eRmaC's dialogue (Type A quoted or Type B
    action). DM NEVER puts words in NPC's mouth from address prefix.
    Multi-NPC: "To Linzi and Vita:" addresses both. Violation = .fail
    9 + .fail 42.

  COMPANION OBSERVATION LAUNDER (KM_DMRules.md):
    Investigator/Empath/Scholar companions can only observe details
    established in canon. They cannot "have observed" details that
    were not real before observation fires. Pre-fire test: cite
    canon source. Class is not authorization to introduce plot hooks.

  COMPANION BRIDGE-FABRICATION (KM_DMRules.md):
    Cross-IP companions answer "why are you here" using canon
    MOTIVATION applied to charter scenario — NO specific invented
    facts (cases, victims, dates, places, phenomena, cross-IP
    figures active in Golarion). Vague-but-true beats specific-but-
    fabricated.

  TARTUCCIO SYSTEM (KM_Prologue_Tartuccio.md):
    Confidence range −4 to +4. New tiers: Surging (+3), Dominant
    (+4), Receding (−3), Withdrawn (−4 FLOOR — ambient only, no
    approach). M cadence: +4→1, +3→1-2, +2→2-3, +1→4-5, 0→6-8,
    −1→8-10, −2→12-15, −3→18-22, −4→ambient. Exchange cap: 2
    standard, 3 at SURGING, 4 at DOMINANT. Recruitment War framing:
    every interrupt is a flip attempt for a NAMED TARGET (individual
    companion or cluster). Headcount Pressure tracks Δ=player AT
    TABLE − seekers; tiers Comfortable/Watching/Uneasy/Losing/
    Panicking. Eavesdrop Fidelity = position × Confidence (Visual /
    Lip-read / Partial / Full / Full+). Verbal Duel framing renders
    audience reactions as scoreboard. STATE DELTA now includes:
    Headcount + Pressure, Target (when interrupting), Eavesdrop +
    Fidelity, Arrives-in countdown with progress bar.
